M2b-diag 2026-08-04 bhead "same old address" collision (0xefec4e70) DIAGNOSED -> STOP/driver. ROOT CAUSE PROVEN from the real file: BHead::old is void* (4B wasm32); uint32_from_uint64_ptr (BLO_core_bhead.hh:124) narrows 64-bit file addr via >>3 (assumes 8-byte alignment). structure_type_inference.blend has two DISTINCT 6-byte DATA blocks 6 bytes apart (0x7fff7f627380 / ...386, sub-8-aligned) differing only in bits1-2 which >>3 discards -> same 32-bit key -> oldnewmap_insert dup -> abort (readfile.cc:2789). Native keeps full 64-bit -> passes. BLAST RADIUS (scan_collide.py): corpus 8/8 = 0 collisions (incl mesh_dense 11306 blocks), this file = 1 -> data-dependent SILENT-corruption risk. KEY: "uint64 map keys" ALONE INSUFFICIENT -> newdataadr looks up 4-byte truncated pointer FIELDS (cast_pointer_64_to_32 >>3, dna_genfile.cc:929); widening only the key makes field lookups miss -> NULL/silent corruption (worse). Correct fix needs pointer-interning surrogate consistent across bhead-read + cast_pointer (couples DNA<->readfile, nested-read stack) => exceeds class; OR wasm64 (sizeof(void*)==8 => native no-truncation path, GOAL-anticipated). RECOMMEND wasm64; wasm32 interner needs ADR. No patch. notes/m2-bhead-pointer-collision.md. upstream untouched by this task.

VK works because its create-info codegen path resolves this differently (same run_preprocessor call at vk_shader_compiler.cc:194 on the full combined — but VK's `code` still carries the annotation / the alias lands). m3-laneA 2026-08-05 round-8 bucket-2 host-shared resource deferral (patch 0050) — THE high-value shader gate; static_shaders 289→481/973 compile (+192), all "syntax error, unexpected IDENTIFIER" (host-shared) failures 601→0. ROOT CAUSE (exact divergence): my resources_declare emitted SSBO/UBO decls INLINE in the resources slot (which precedes the resolved `code`), so a buffer whose element type is `<Struct>_host_shared_` (from info.buffer_typename) referenced that identifier BEFORE the shader-translation tool's `#define <Struct>_host_shared_ <Struct>` alias (emitted at the struct-def site inside `code`) → shaderc "unexpected IDENTIFIER". VK does NOT emit inline: vk_shader.cc:408-419 wraps each decl in `#define CREATE_INFO_RES_<freq>_<info> \ …` and the translation tool (shader_tool/processor.cc:371 get_create_info_placeholder) plants `#ifdef CREATE_INFO_RES_<freq>_<info> … #endif` in the resolved code AFTER the struct defs+aliases, so the macro expands where `_host_shared_` resolves. run_preprocessor (gpu_shader_preprocessor.cc Preprocessor) is a pure macro/directive engine — it RESOLVES+ERASES the alias `#define`, it never regenerates it; the alias must already be in-scope at reference time. FIX (wgpu_shader.cc, mirror VK exactly): resources_declare now defers each resource into CREATE_INFO_RES_<PASS|BATCH|GEOMETRY>_<res.info_name> macros via new print_resource_deferred (assign_if_different grouping), keeping the dense unique bindings (0038) and WGSL storage-qualifier promotion (0036). Same mechanism Blender's own preprocessor uses; general — also covers Python shaders (frontend injects the CREATE_INFO_RES_PASS_pyGPU_Shader placeholder, gpu_shader.cc:290). bf_gpu+blender_test green; shader_python_graphic/compute + shader_ssbo_binding + storage_buffer_* still PASS (8/8, no regression); patch 0050 reverse-applies clean; upstream HEAD fbe6228. New top remaining buckets (per-shader first error, 492 fail): 100 tint ProgramFromIR "extension not allowed in current environment" + 93 tint ReadIR "SPIR-V capability not supported" + 54 tint ReadIR "store to point_size is not a constant" + 32 tint textureSample + 32 tint nan-f32 (bucket 3) + 25 non-uniform-control-flow — i.e. the tail is now Tint SPIR-V reader/writer feature gaps, NOT GLSL-dialect. m3-laneA 2026-08-05 round-8 bucket-3 (nan f32) ROOT-CAUSED, NOT landed (blocker escalation) — 30 shaders (polyline/overlay_armature/workbench_shadow/eevee_shadow/gpencil/edit_curve/motion_path). Root cause: NAN_FLT sentinel = `#define NAN_FLT uintBitsToFloat(0x7FC00000u)` (gpu_shader_utildefines_lib.glsl:20), used as `gl_Position = float4(NAN_FLT)` to cull verts. glslang's FRONT-END const-folds uintBitsToFloat(literal) into a NaN `OpConstant %float` (independent of the optimizer — our compiler already runs optimize=false), the Tint SPIR-V reader ingests it as a NaN f32 core::constant, and the WGSL writer (ProgramFromIR, ir_to_program.cc:990 b.Expr(ValueAs<f32>())) builds a float-LITERAL AST node that fails resolution because WGSL forbids NaN/Inf literals → "tint ProgramFromIR: value nan cannot be represented as f32". No clean lever inside lane-A ownership: tint reader/writer Options expose no nan flag (spirv/reader/common/options.h has only sampler_mappings/dump/asserts), there is no core-IR nan-legalization transform, and disabling optimization does nothing (glslang folds at parse). ESCALATION (driver): cleanest fix is a WebGPU variant of NAN_FLT that survives to WGSL as `bitcast<f32>(0x7fc00000u)` (which WGSL permits) — but gpu_shader_utildefines_lib.glsl is a shared shader source (read-only, all backends) and keying it on `#ifdef GPU_WEBGPU` is impossible today because standard_defines (gpu/intern/gpu_shader.cc:169-182) has NO GPU_BACKEND_WEBGPU case — it hits `default: BLI_assert_msg(false)` and defines NO backend macro (separate latent gap: `#ifdef GPU_VULKAN/METAL/OPENGL` are all false under WebGPU and there is no GPU_WEBGPU to branch on). In-lane alternative = a custom Tint-IR pass (ReadIR→walk→replace NaN/Inf f32 scalar+vec-splat constants with core::ir::Bitcast from a u32 const→ProgramFromIR) in wgpu_shader_compiler.cc; feasible with the linked Tint IR API but non-trivial (vector-splat handling, per-use insertion) and risks the banked 481 — bounded to a future round, not attempted this round to protect the bucket-2 gain. bucket-2 patch 0050 intact; static_shaders 481/973; bf_gpu+blender_test green; upstream HEAD fbe6228.

The wgpuInstanceAddRef unaligned trap is a GARBAGE/misaligned instance pointer, empirically shown in real Chrome (trace getInstance() instance_=0x11 %8=1), not a misaligned atomic member. m3-gpu 2026-08-06 round-14 patch 0086 — integer-sampler filtered-sampling → textureLoad (M3.F11c). WGSL forbids textureSample/textureSampleLevel on integer textures, so texture()/textureLod() on a usampler/isampler (Tint "no matching call to textureSample(texture_2d<u32>,...)") broke 37 shaders (32 textureSample fragment + 5 textureSampleLevel = compute-stage texture() lowered to explicit-lod). GL forces integer textures to NEAREST regardless of sampler state, so the faithful equivalent is texture(s,uv)==texelFetch(s, ivecN(uv*textureSize(s,0)), 0). New rewrite_integer_sampler_sampling() on the fully-preprocessed per-stage GLSL: recovers integer-sampler names+dim from their [iu]sampler{1,2,3}D decls (resource decls + fn params both; struct members like srt.stencil_tx / color_band.flag_tx and fn-param samplers like object_id_tx all resolve to the bare name at that point — VERIFIED by dumping resolved sources), then rewrites texture(NAME,uv[,bias])/textureLod(NAME,uv,lod) → texelFetch(NAME, ivecN((uv)*vecN(textureSize(NAME,lod))), lod), .r/.x swizzles preserved (texelFetch on usampler returns uvec4). Covers workbench_resolve object-id/curvature (usampler2D), workbench_volume flag_tx (usampler3D), workbench_effect_outline, select_debug, compositor_realize_on_domain (isampler2D). VERIFIED native Dawn: textureSample 32→0 + textureSampleLevel 5→0, total 836→875 (+37 compile), ZERO newly-failing. Runtime held: blend 12/12, immediate 2/2, multi_viewport PASS, framebuffer clear+cube 5/5, texture 64/66 (2 pre-existing depth-upload). STACK: 0086 stacks on 0084 (both wgpu_shader.cc); reverse-apply order 0086→0084 reconstructs pristine (verified). Semantics equivalence (integer nearest-fetch) documented in-patch. upstream HEAD fbe6228.
m3-gpu 2026-08-06 round-14 patch 0087 — 1D-array sampler → 2D emulation (M3.F11d, textureDimensions). ROOT CAUSE: WGSL has no 1D-array sampled texture; Tint degrades Dim=1D/Arrayed to texture_1d (drops the array), so textureSize/textureDimensions returns scalar u32 while the shader used the 2-component result → "textureDimensions: call result type vec2<u32> does not match builtin return type u32" (25 shaders: workbench_prepass_mesh/ptcloud_*_texture, all via node_tex_tile_lookup(sampler1DArray) UDIM lookup). The sampler is passed by function parameter in workbench_image_lib.glsl (off-limits), so a resource-only type change would break the call — fixed instead at the resolved-source layer. A 1D-array IS a 2D texture whose height is the layer count (identical texelFetch/textureSize ivec2 signatures): rewrite_1d_array_samplers() swaps the sampler1DArray type token → sampler2D everywhere (resource decls + fn params, one suffix replace covers i/u/plain prefixes) and remaps filtered texture(NAME, vec2(u,layer)) → texture(NAME, vec2(u, (layer+0.5)/height)) so bilinear hits the exact row centre (no cross-layer blend — GL never filters across array layers) while u keeps linear filtering; texelFetch(ivec2) is already identical so UDIM is exact. Interface map infer_view_dimension(e1DArray)→e2D to match. VERIFIED native Dawn: textureDimensions 25→0, total 875→900 (+25), ZERO newly-failing; runtime blend 12 + compute + immediate 2 + multi_viewport = 24/24 held. STACK on wgpu_shader.cc: 0084<0086<0087; reverse chain 0087→0086→0084 reconstructs pristine (verified, both files). upstream HEAD fbe6228.

(1) _sha3 + _md5/_sha1/_blake2 RE-ENABLED in scripts/deps/python.sh: their in-tree Hacl/_blake2 sources compile DIRECTLY into libpython (only _sha2 links a prebuilt libHacl_Hash_SHA2.a; M2.3's "Hacl won't build standalone -> disable" note was BACKWARDS). Pyodide ships no _multiprocessing and patches the consumer, but bl_pkg is pinned/read-only -> installed a thread-backed pure-python shim scripts/deps/python-shims/_multiprocessing.py into the harvested stdlib (name=None sidesteps the resource_tracker/_posixshmem path). gpu-r17-YFLIP 2026-08-06 **M4.T14a present-path Y-flip FIXED — Blender window composites UPRIGHT in a real Chrome tab.** ADR-005 clip-flip (0052) is correct/load-bearing for offscreen but over-flips the window (WebGPU NDC is +Y-up like GL; the surface is browser-composited with no readback). Fix (patch 0096, 7 webgpu files): clip-space Y sign is a pipeline-overridable constant gpu_clip_y_sign (WGSL override @id 1000, default -1.0f); PipelineInfo::flip_y (in the cache key) is false only for the surface-backed window backbuffer (WGPUContext::is_window_backbuffer), where the override -> +1.0f and the front-face swap is dropped. Offscreen pipelines leave the override unset -> byte-identical. ROOT: the storage-BUFFER strip for C1 already ships in TWO places (wgpu_shader_interface_map.cc:213-237, wgpu_shader.cc:2354-2362) but the storage-IMAGE analog is ABSENT in both (imap StorageImage case :239-241 passes full vis; explicit-layout :2360 tests only buffer.type==Storage). worker 2026-08-09T18:30Z r49 DESIGN-ONLY (M5 sync-readback contract, gpu-sync-readback-windowed deferral, no code fence): delivered notes/m5-sync-readback-contract-design.md. MECHANISM VERDICT: the sync path returns zeros because a same-thread blocking wait halts the very WM-worker event loop that must run to deliver the WebGPU mapAsync completion - NOT the F9-D prediction that the worker could just Atomics.wait. Two proven facts compound: (a) the port blocking WaitAny is asyncify-only and returns immediately unfulfilled under ADR-006 (WGPUTexture::read wgpu_texture.cc:1277, Buffer::read wgpu_buffer.cc:164); (b) even a real blocking wait deadlocks - the map callback is a microtask on this same worker loop and the per-thread JS-object table (ADR-007) forbids offloading it. Decisive lever proven twice: AllowSpontaneous + return-to-loop (patches 0117, 0125); ProcessEvents delivers 0 completions/30s (r31). PROBE (read-only, tree b7dd8f2, BW_DIAG unset): reconfirmed r25 s2 first-hand - render.opengl and screen.screenshot both Constant 0/255 while the CDP compositor control painted (avg 61.9); table-OOB did NOT reproduce this run (loop survived), consistent with a timing-dependent callback-lifetime race. CENSUS: 7 M5 caller families over 2 seams; critical few = C1 object pick (StorageBuffer read, select_instance.hh:271-272) + C2 legacy element/box select (FB read, draw_select_buffer.cc:104); C3 depth (view3d_draw.cc:2426) rides along. RECOMMENDED: kick-on-request/latch-on-tick/consume-on-settle with a harness-driven wait_for_gpu_idle barrier; event-simulate compatible because the operator trace records calls not tick timing and the settle barrier is what the harness already needs. REJECTED option (b) sync busy-wait+ProcessEvents pump (falsified 3 ways: r31 0-completions, emscripten loop non-reentrancy, Atomics.wait halts the delivering loop); JSPI/asyncify rejected by ADR-006. TABLE-OOB: same root (undeliverable sync map future), separate manifestation (WaitAnyOnly lambda captures stack by ref, fires after read() returns -> stale table slot); subsumed by the kick-then-consume shape, plus a small standalone safety patch M5.readback-callback-lifetime. ACCEPTANCE: event-simulate click-pick on the default cube -> ACTIVE=Cube, one view3d.select(mx,my) in trace matching oracle, deterministic, no-crash on repeat. EFFORT: L-A backend primitive (fenced webgpu lane, promote wgpu_diag_readback + Buffer::read async sibling + lifetime fix, ~2-4 patches MEDIUM), L-B GPU async/poll API (SMALL-MEDIUM), L-C caller conversion + settle barrier (MEDIUM-LARGE), L-D M5 harness (separate). r53 2026-08-09T11:18Z M5 L-A PRODUCTION READBACK PRIMITIVE COMPLETE (patch 0133). Promotes the twice-proven AllowSpontaneous tick-pumped readback (0117/0125) from a BW_DIAG-only diagnostic to a production kick/latch/settle primitive at BOTH backend seams (WGPUTexture::read, webgpu::Buffer::read) behind one heap-owned per-device registry (new wgpu_readback.{cc,hh}). CONTRACT (per r49 design notes/m5-sync-readback-contract-design.md sec 3.1): a read() KICKS a CopyTextureToBuffer/CopyBufferToBuffer + MapAsync(AllowSpontaneous) into a heap Pending record (registry owns the staging buffer; the callback captures ONLY a uint64 ticket, never a caller-stack ref); the completion LATCHES tight device bytes on a later WM-loop tick; a later read of the same source CONSUMES the latched bytes (kick-now, fill-one-tick-late), first-call-unsettled returns the conservative interim (zeros/empty). LIFETIME FIX (M5.readback-callback-lifetime, r25 table-OOB): on wasm the old MapAsync(WaitAnyOnly)+WaitAny [&]-capture lambda (which could fire after read() returned through a reused wasm table slot) is #ifdef __EMSCRIPTEN__-compiled OUT; it does not exist in the wasm binary. NATIVE preserved EXACTLY: both seams keep the verbatim blocking-WaitAny path under #else; wgpu_readback.cc is a 336-byte stub on native.
